1. Your betting rules, unchanged: +$20.75
The rules (as they ran until Oct 16): bets at 54¢ to 98¢; since Oct 17 5:44 PM only 54¢ to 96¢ (every bet on this page is $5).
• First half, 1 minute before halftime, score unchanged since 45:00: 0-0 or 1-1 → tie; 1-0, 0-1, 2-1, 1-2 → team ahead. No bet with a red card, in cups or Brasileirao C.
• Full game, score at 90:00 with no goal since: 0-0, 1-1, 2-2 → tie, 3 minutes before the end; 1-0 or 0-1 → team ahead, 1 minute before the end. No bet on 2-1, 5+ goals, a 2-goal lead, or a tie with a red card.
• 3+ goal lead at 90:00, 5 or fewer minutes added, 1 minute before the end → team ahead to win by more than (lead minus a half).
Each bet is held to the end.

3. Late-goal bets alone: +$149.50
The logic tested and tuned last night: only in 2nd-half added time (90'+). When a goal is seen (a result's price jumps 15¢+ in 3 seconds on 15+ trades in 2 seconds, 8× the game's normal pace, while another result drops), buy $5 of what the goal made likely, at the live price. Four separate bets: scoring team to win, tie (after an equalizer), scoring team to win by more (5¢ jump, 10 trades), more total goals. Another goal → sell and buy the new side. Price falls back to where it was (goal overturned) → sell.

Tap a line to open it. Totals on every line; the last level lists each bet.
